- Fix: Re-slice in tools/art/effects.mjs. Take each frame from a wider window (for example 32x30, centred), keep only the connected pixels whose bbox centre lies in that frame's own cell, and re-bake. Alternatively, hand-fix frames 2-7. Check fx_fireball the same way.

### vfx-9 [unchecked] Half-resolution VFX glow layers are blown up 2x with nearest sampling: a coarse 2-art-pixel dither checker
- defect / code - public/js/game/fxart.js:190-194 (VfxBurst.drawGlow: place(..., gl.w*2, gl.h*2) with smoothing off)
- Evidence: tech-extra/w10-checker.png (Thunderstorm, weapons-10.png row 5 tile 2): 4-screen-px checker cells next to 2 px bolt pixels. The dithered glow frames are in atlas-vfx-2.png (vfx_zap, vfx_zap_big, vfx_meteor, vfx_ult glow rows). The checker is also visible in the Thunderstorm tiles and in Stormglaive in weapons-fusions.png.
- The half-size dithered glows are doubled with nearest sampling. That draws a checkerboard at twice the art pixel size round every zap, meteor and ult flash. It reads as grid noise, not light.
- Fix: Turn on ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled for the glow blit only and restore it after. Alternatively, bake the glow frames at full size without dither.

### vfx-10 [unchecked] Reaction words (SCALD / COMBUST / SHATTER) are buried under their own splash numbers and can land on the hero
- defect / code - public/js/game/reactions.js:52-55 (each splash hitEnemy adds a plain number), :71-72 (word at e.y - e.h - 6); public/js/game/numbers.js:170-199 (text(): font 'normal', same lane, no nearHero/CLEAR check), :258-300 (one draw loop in list order)
- Evidence: Both lenses. reactions.png:
- SCALD at 0.03-0.35 s sits inside a 3x3 grid of '81's.
- COMBUST at 0.35 s (about 1230-1350, 390-420) reads '2CO2US20'.
- SHATTER is crossed by '21 21 24'.

tech-extra/b1080-hero.png (busy-1080p-knight_forest.png at about 700-790, 435-460): a SHATTER word lies across the Knight's legs and HP bar. In the busy 1080p frame the SHATTER words are small blue-on-blue.
- The word is the only signal that teaches the G1 reactions (hints.js listens to it). It is drawn in the small normal font at the same height as the 8 splash numbers, rises more slowly, and later hits draw over it. It also ignores the hero's clear zone. Numbers on every hit is settled; the word's placement is not.
- Fix: 1. In Numbers.draw, draw all numbers first, then the words.
2. Draw words in the bold logo font at about 1.3x crit size, with a 1-px #181425 plate.
3. Spawn them at e.y - e.h - 14..20 with no horizontal jitter, rising slower than numbers.
4. Spawn same-react() splash numbers below the head (+6).
5. If the word's spawn point is within ~16 px of the hero, lift it above his head (p.y - 30).

### vfx-11 [unchecked] Gale Horn and Tempest show no gust: 26-46 one-pixel particles
- defect / code - public/js/game/crowdfx.js:44-50 (gust(): size-1 particles, life 0.25-0.5 s, no shaped effect); public/js/game/weapons.js:793-799
- Evidence: Both lenses. weapons-14 rows 1-2: no wind shape in any frame, only foes being shoved. Footprint at max level: gale 176 px, tempest 184 px (0.17% of a 105,600 px frame), the smallest of any shared weapon, against a 10,000+ median for evolved weapons. Tempest is a 6,068-damage evolution.
- A knockback weapon only satisfies if you see the push. Here the horde jumps back with no cause on screen, and Tempest looks like nothing equipped.
- Fix: On each blast:
- Add an art-grid wave front: slashFrame(GALE_R*s.area, s.arc*2, aimIdx, f, 6, ['#ffffff','#c0f0ff','#2ce8f5','#124e89']) over 0.25 s, or a crescent band of 3-5 streaks 2 art px thick.
- For Tempest, use a ringSprite expanding to GALE_R, plus a second slower ring and leaf/dust particles.
- Add a faint 20% dithered cone fill for 0.08 s.
- Raise the streak particles to size 2 with P.SHRINK.

Draw the streaks from hash2 of the blast id, keeping the Math.random count unchanged.

### vfx-12 [unchecked] Overgrowth (Druid ultimate) reads as nothing: 70 dark root sprites, unlit at night
- defect / code - public/js/game/effects.js:1821-1880 (class Overgrowth: draw only, no drawGlow, no g.light; vines = WEAPON_SPRITES.root 12x16 at random screen points)
- Evidence: ult-overgrowth-frenzy.png top row and art-extra/overgrowth-crop.png: small maroon-brown twigs on dark grass, and only the numbers show anything happened. Every other ult has a light or flash beat.
- The screen-wide ultimate reuses the Snaring Roots sprite at 1x, with no glow or light, below ground value at night. The Druid's biggest moment is the least visible ultimate in the game.
- Fix: 1. In drawGlow, add a green screen pulse (#63c74d, 0.35 alpha, fading over 0.4 s).
2. Add g.light(x, y, 40, 'green', 0.6) per vine cluster, capped at about 12.
3. Give the vine tips an unlit 'em' key, or use World Root's larger vine sprite.
4. Burst leaf particles as each vine surfaces.
5. Draw the root grip in front of held foes, with a 1-px green rim.

### vfx-13 [unchecked] Recruit melee cuts are flat procedural fans that cover the foes they hit
- defect / code - public/js/game/fxsprites.js:93-140 (drawSlash: thickness up to 0.66*radius, alpha 1, 4 flat bands); users: expansion/weapons/footman-behavior.js:50-57, :106 (BRONZE shock at SHOCK_ARC 2.25 rad), praetorian-behavior.js:86, swordbearer-behavior.js:82, wraith-behavior.js:77
- Evidence: - art-extra/footman-arc.png: a sandy 129-degree fan in 3 flat bands, hiding the skeleton behind it.
- art-extra/wraith-arc.png: flat lavender pie slices.
- weapons-03 rows 5-6 (Praetorian) and weapons-04 rows 5-6 (Swordbearer): cardboard wedges.
- Compare atlas-vfx-1 vfx_slash_46 / vfx_holy_68.
- The original heroes' cuts moved to the baked Sprite Fusion family; the recruits still use the fallback generator. Opaque fat sectors read as UI shapes, not motion, and hide the struck foe for 0.16-0.23 s.
- Fix: Preferred: bake the recruit cuts through tools/vfx/bake.mjs, recoloured vfx_slash/vfx_chop in the STEEL, BRONZE, GOLD and SHADE palettes.

Otherwise, in drawSlash:

- Dither the body past u > 0.3.

### vfx-14 [unchecked] Statuses with no mark: slowed, rooted, stunned, afraid, poisoned and bleeding look like a plain foe; Exposed is invisible
- polish / code - public/js/game/enemies.js:1023-1028 (drawEmissive draws only brand, curse and confuse marks); public/js/game/reactions.js:16-19 (exposed()); reviews/2026-11-21-gameplay-review.md:72 (status pip, part of G1, not built)
- Evidence: Both lenses. status-marks.png, all rows: poisoned, bleeding, slowed 50%, stunned, rooted and afraid are pixel-identical to plain (see also tech-extra/st-marks.png). Only frozen, branded, cursed, confused, lulled and champion read. DoTs show only through occasional sparks.
- G1 made states into setups that other weapons cash in (Exposed: +25% crit on held or slowed foes; Combust needs poison). The player can't see which foes are set up, so the combo system is invisible on the field.
- Fix: Add 3x3 art-px pips in drawEmissive, unlit, over the head in the shared mark stack, at most 2 per foe:
- slow: blue down-chevron #2ce8f5, or under the feet
- root: bracket #9e8c60
- stun: two circling white pixels or a #fee761 star, like confuse's sparks
- poison: green drop #9ee562
- bleed: red drop #e43b44
- fear: white '!' or a sweat-drop pixel

For Exposed, add a 1-frame gold rim pulse every 0.6 s. Skip horde pips above about 150 foes on screen if cost matters.

### vfx-15 [unchecked] Hex hourglass floats ~6 px above the head and overlaps the Smite brand when both are on
- polish / code - public/js/game/enemies.js:1044-1046 (brand spans Y-11S..Y-2S), :1053-1064 (hourglass rows top+(dy-8)S with top=Y-3S, so Y-11S..Y-6S)
- Evidence: tech-extra/st-marks.png: the brand sits 2 px over the skull and the hourglass about 6-7 art px over it, detached. By the code both start at Y-11S on the same centre line, so a branded and cursed foe (Paladin or Beacon with Hex) draws the hourglass over the brand diamond.
- The two head marks use unrelated offsets, so one floats loose and the two collide when stacked.
- Fix: Give the marks one stack. Brand at Y-11S..Y-2S. The hourglass at Y-7S..Y-2S when alone, and to the right of the brand (cx + 6S) when both are on.

### vfx-16 [unchecked] Raise Dead allies are enemy skeletons with green eyes
- polish / art - public/js/game/minions.js:16 (LOOKS.skeleton sprite 'minion', glow '#63c74d' at 0.22, :283-288); public/js/art/monsters.js (minion rows reuse the skeleton)

### vfx-17 [unchecked] Fury comet is a placeholder: white square head and square tail, wrong colours, lands on the orb's % text
- polish / code - public/js/game/hud.js:709-734 (tail of fillRect squares #be4a2f/#f6757a; head = 8x8 #feae34 at 0.45 + 5x5 #ffffff); target (ocx, ocy) = the orb centre, where the % label is drawn earlier at :699

The fury orb pickup itself is a magenta gem (#b55088).

End the arc at the orb rim (ocy - ORB_R*0.6), or draw the % label after the comets, and let cometHit's ring do the impact.

### vfx-18 [unchecked] Ultimate name label is struck through by the cut-in band's lower rule
- polish / code - public/js/game/hud.js:691-697 (ultLabel drawn whenever p.ult) and :749-754 (ultLabelY), drawn after the band (:386 then :390), against :841-842 (band rule at by + bh - 3)
- Evidence: Both lenses. art-extra/deadrise-banner.png and tech-extra/cut-label.png (from ult-deadrise-sanctum.png at about (560-796, 355-375), 0.40 s): the #f6757a rule runs through the HUD's 'ARMY OF THE DEAD' label, reading as 'ARMY_OF_THE_DEAD', with the banner's own title duplicated right under it.
- From about 0.2 s, while the cut-in is still on screen, the HUD label duplicates the banner's title and collides with its edge line. That is a layout collision at the most cinematic moment.
- Fix: Skip the ult label while g.ultLaunch is active (or fade it in after T.dur). Alternatively, place it using ultLabelY with a check against the band's [by, by+bh] rows.

### vfx-19 [unchecked] Ultimate cut-in: portrait at a fractional scale, long names squashed below 1:1
- defect / code - public/js/game/hud.js:845-846 (ps = (bh - 6) / pf.height), :847-848 (ns = Math.min(1, W*0.62/name.width))
- Evidence: Portrait scales at H=222: knight 1.58, mage 1.30, eanasir 1.13, berserker 1.73. At 1080p (H=270): knight 1.94, eanasir 1.39. tech-extra/cut-knight.png and tech-extra/cut-hunter.png mix 1- and 2-UI-px cells.

Logo widths (tech-extra/logow.mjs): 'ARMY OF THE DEAD' is 360 px, giving ns 0.68 at W=396 and 0.83 at W=480. 'SHADOW DANCE' gives 0.88 and 'ARROW STORM' 0.96. Nearest sampling drops 4-32% of the logo's rows and columns.
- The hero pose is resampled at 1.1-2.1x on the UI canvas, so some pixel rows double and others don't. Long ult names are downscaled with nearest sampling and their strokes break up.
- Fix: Use an integer portrait factor and fit the band to it: k = Math.max(1, Math.round((H*0.26 - 6)/pf.height)), bh = k*pf.height + 6. Never draw the logo below 1:1. For long names, use the bold textCanvas at 2x, or split the name onto two lines.

### vfx-20 [unchecked] Damage numbers on breakable boxes pile over the hero
- taste / owner - public/js/game/game.js:1461 (numbers.add for every non-noNum blow, props included)
- Evidence: Both lenses. pickups-boxes-chest.png at 0.38-0.62 s: stacks of 51/53/54/55/109/100 over and beside the Knight, in a scene with no monsters. The '109' crit spins over his body, and '55 55' overlap into a garbled '55ʳ5'.
- Barrels, crates, urns and lanterns get the full monster treatment: pop flash, crit spin and merge. The numbers carry no information on scenery and crowd the 0.2 s loot pop. Whether boxes count under the owner's 'numbers on every hit' rule is the owner's call.
- Fix: If the owner agrees, add && !e.prop to the numbers condition at game.js:1461, keeping the flash and debris. Or show one small plain number only on the breaking blow.

## hud

Both critics agree: the HUD is not pixel perfect yet, but the base is strong. At 1280x720, 1920x1080 and on phone, the in-run HUD is coherent and well made, and these parts should stay as they are: the per-pixel liquid orbs with crisp ringSprite rims, the bronze and iron WEAPONS/POWERS plates, the edge markers that steer round every HUD box, the notched boss bar with its drain trail and gold name, and the whole-number text scaling (banners x2, SLAIN BY at 2 or 1, the NEW BEST pop). The level-up, chest and pause screens are clean at 640x360 and 480x270 (h-bossbar-640x360 and c1280-04 are genuinely good). Three faults show on every ultimate at every resolution: the cut-in band is drawn under the hero's HP bar, the edge pointers, the banners and the ult label; the cut-in pose is drawn at a fractional scale (Knight x2.67 at 720p, x1.94 at 1080p, with 2-px and 3-px pixels measured side by side); and the logo font is shrunk by fractions whenever a title does not fit ('ARMY OF THE DEAD' x0.83 at 1080p, 'YOU HAVE FALLEN' and EVOLUTION! x0.56-0.87 on small windows). On short or narrow windows (320x240 and smaller, 580x216), the results screen does not reflow and overprints itself. Its last line also sits on the panel border at every size, and it prints '× 2.4000000000000004'. Banners, compact level-up and merchant cards, the level-up footer, chest COLLECT and the crypt map overprint each other or the plates, and the letterbox slices the orbs. At 240x210 the loadout covers the hero. On finish: the orb sheen makes a grey disc on empty glass, the locked orb is see-through, the crypt map is translucent and has no frame, the chest still shows the legacy blue slot strip, and the pointers, stick, chevron and ring warning are anti-aliased shapes next to flat pills. Smaller items: the phone pause/MAP buttons blur at odd widths (W=633), the LUCKY!/chain stamps are rotated into uneven pixels, icons are upscaled three different ways, and pale day-map text relies on its outline alone. Every fix is local, and most are a few lines. Two items are out of scope and only flagged. First, the world camera draws at cam.S = view.S*0.8 (game.js:2787), a fractional world scale; that is for the world critics or the owner. Second, the pause panel's 320 px cap (gamescreen.js:1525) cuts off 'Guardian Blad…' at 1280x720; raising it to min(W-16, 400) would fix that. Other session's work: ResultsOverlay, LevelUpOverlay and ChestOverlay live in public/js/ui/gamescreen.js, which has an uncommitted 3-line demo edit that does not touch the cited lines. profile.js also has uncommitted changes, at other lines. hud.js, loadout.js, dungeon.js and trophy.js have no pending edits. Pose measurements come from public/assets/heroes-approved, not the WIP public/assets/heroes/*.js. In the evidence below, paths are relative to C:/Users/I/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/015cd6aa7d087c29/bb69f979-5b65-4b71-a433-ff8642911dab/scratchpad/gfx-review/hud/. There, art-extra/ holds the art critic's crops and the poseh/logow/logoh scripts, and tech-extra/ holds the tech critic's crops and the sizes.mjs/toprow.mjs scripts.

### hud-1 [confirmed] Results panel (death/victory) overprints itself below ~360 art px tall, and its last line sits on the panel border at every size
- bug / code - public/js/ui/gamescreen.js:2023-2060 (cap/need/ph), 2067-2095 (rows and weapons anchored from the top), 2097-2120 (gy, notes, chips anchored from the bottom), 2173-2187 (NEXT at y+ph-20, armory at y+ph-11), 1987 (logo scale). The other session's demo edit does not touch these lines. [both critics: art + tech]
- Evidence: out-chrome/c240-23-death-recap.png: 'Firebomb Flask 2/2' over the Excalibur row (y~485), 'Collected 70 × 1…' over Guardian Blades (y~560), '+107 GOLD' over Hunting Bow (y~615), NEXT over Fireball (y~685), the armory line over Lightning Strike (y~720). c320-33-victory.png: the chips and 'OATHS 6 · SIR ALDRIC'S BEST' over 'Gold found' and the Longsword/Guardian Blades rows. c580-23: the UNLOCKED row (y~265) runs across the right-hand weapons column (x600-800). Space vs need: 240x210 136 vs 235; 320x240 154 vs 200-235 (46+ px overlap); 580x216 130 vs 171; only 640x360 fits (274 vs 171). Bottom line: drawn at y+ph-11 with GLYPH_H 9 plus 1 px of outline, so it lands on the trim (art-extra/victory-bottom.png; c1280-33, c1280-23, c1920-33, cphone-23). c1280-23: the panel stops at ~y250 of 360, leaving 110 px empty. c1920-33 UNSAVED: base=178 leaves a ~100 px empty band.
- The panel has two independent stacks. The stat rows and weapons go down from the top, and the chips, notes, gold, NEXT and armory lines go up from the bottom (gy = y+ph-51-nNear*10). ph is capped at cap = H-52-logo height, but nothing reflows when need > cap, so the stacks overlap. fit = Math.max(1, …) forces a chip row even when there is no room. In side mode the chips centre across the full width, under the weapons column. There is no bottom margin, and the panel never spreads out when there is room.
- Fix: (1) Margins: draw the armory line at y+ph-13 and NEXT at y+ph-22, and add 2 to base and need(). (2) Lay the panel out top-down from one ty cursor with a drop order. When tight > cap, drop in this order: 'Damage dealt'; then show weapons as two columns (when !side and pw>=220), or the top 3 plus '+N more', or a single 'MVP: Excalibur 4980 · 58/s' line; then wounds and near-lock lines (trim notes to 1); then page chips via chipPages, and finally page STATS/WEAPONS. In side mode, start the chips at max(rows bottom, weapons bottom)+4. When cap allows, add 3 px gaps between sections. (3) UNSAVED: use base-40. (4) When (W-20)/L.width < 1, do not shrink the logo; see the fractional-scale finding.
- Reproduce sceptic (real, bug): The finding is real, but the headline "below ~360 art px" overstates how far it reaches. What I confirmed: 1) Overlap. The code matches the description. The rows and weapons stack goes down from y. The gold, notes and chips stack goes up from gy = y+ph-51-nNear*10. ph = min(cap, …) and nothing reflows when tight+unH > cap. fit = Math.max(1, …) forces one chip row. In side mode, popping 'Damage dealt' frees nothing once there are 4 or more weapons, because max(rows*10, nw*15) is set by the weapons.    I re-captured in real Chrome on :3017 (fresh profile, Knight; victory on hell with 3 oaths, and a death on meadow) and saved the UI canvas at art resolution:    - 445x250 (1334x750 window): the chip row prints over Fireball and Lightning Strike.    - 480x230 (960x460 window): chips over Bosses slain / Gold found / Hunting Bow, and 'The Ranger is unlocked!' over Lightning Strike.    - 320x240 (640x480 window): UNLOCKED over Throwing Axe / Hunting Bow, 'Collected…' over Fireball, +35 GOLD over Lightning Strike.    The critic's c240, c320 and c580 shots show the same thing.    It does NOT happen at the usual sizes. These all fit cleanly with the heaviest first-win content: 480x270 (1080p fullscreen), 480x267, 640x300, 640x320, 640x325, 683x325, and the 320x324 half-screen docked window. So in side mode (W >= 346) it starts at about H <= 255, and in stacked mode at about H <= 300. That is reachable (small windows with an inner height of about 450-510 px at dpr 1, 4:3 640x480, 800x450 -> 400x225), but most desktops and phones never hit it. 2) Bottom line on the trim. Confirmed in pixels at 480x270 (verify/bottom2-480x270.png). The armory line sits at y+ph-11, so the descender row of g/p/y lands on the panel's trim row (ph-3; the panel skin's middle column is trim at h-3, ink at h-2, clear at h-1). Its outline notches the gold line either side. Most armory lines contain 'gold', so this shows at every size. 3) Not defects: the empty band under the buttons at 640x320/640x360 and the UNSAVED gap are taste. The 1920 UNSAVED shot is a capture artefact: the hero chosen was locked ('That mode is locked for this hero'). Why the proposed fix is not sound as written: - Part (1) moves only NEXT (to ph-22) and the armory line (to ph-13). The near-lock lines stay at y+ph-20-(nNear-i)*10, so one near lock at ph-30 would overlap the new NEXT by a glyph row. - Part (2) is a full top-down rewrite with a five-step drop order and paging of STATS/WEAPONS. That is far from minimal. Its 3 px gaps and base-40 for UNSAVED are taste extras. Side note (a separate bug, not this finding): every oath capture prints 'Gold 900 × 2.4000000000000004'. goldParts (gamescreen.js:1691) uses the raw float r.multiplier. Files: C:/Users/I/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/015cd6aa7d087c29/bb69f979-5b65-4b71-a433-ff8642911dab/scratchpad/gfx-review/hud/verify/v-victory-1334x750.png, v-victory-960x460.png, v-death-640x480.png, v-death-960x460.png, v-victory-1920x1080.png, bottom2-480x270.png (script: verify/results.mjs). Better fix: (a) Bottom margin. Move the whole bottom stack up 2 px from one anchor. In ResultsOverlay draw, set const foot = y + ph - 2 and use foot wherever the code now uses y + ph for gy (foot-51-nNear*10), the nearLines rows (foot-20-(nNear-i)*10), ny (foot-20) and the armory/volatile line (foot-11). Add 2 to need() and to base. An equivalent change is to draw panel(ctx, x, y, pw, ph + 2, …) with cap -= 2 and by = min(H-20, y+ph+8). Do not move only NEXT and the armory line. (b) Overlap. After tight, unH and nNear are computed, let the weapons give way before anything overprints. While tight + unH > cap and nw > 1, decrement nw and recompute need(). In side mode do this only while nw*15 > rows*10. Draw the top nw weapons by damage (MVP kept) and turn the last row into '+k more' when some are hidden. Pop 'Damage dealt' only when the rows column is the taller one; in side mode with 4 or more weapons, popping it frees nothing today. Then trim the non-wound notes to what still fits; today only the wounds are trimmed. Keep the owner's rule of at least one chip row, but only after weapons and notes have yielded. (c) Leave the empty band at 640x360 and the UNSAVED base alone (taste). Leave the logo scale to its own finding.
- Intent sceptic (real, bug): The finding is real. Nothing in the reviews folder settles it, and the code comments treat overprint as a defect. Line 2036 says the rows give way before the unlocked do, and lines 2050-2052 record a fix made on 27 October because unlocks were pushed onto the sixth weapon at 480x270. results.test.js does not check whether the layout fits. I reproduced it on a saved run with a fresh profile at the most common size, 1920x1080 full screen (480x270 art px, S=4). On a first victory the page-3/6 chip row prints over the sixth weapon row: Lighthouse Lantern lands on Lightning Strike, and the '3/6' tail lands on its damage figures. On that screen and on the death screen, the last line ('Magnetism I · 50 gold…' / '+6 gold to Magnetism I') sits on the bottom trim, with its descenders touching the border. Why it happens at 480x270: cap = 270-52-36 = 182 and side mode is on. With 6 weapons, tight = 16+90+55 = 161 before any notes. A first win adds 3 notes (oath best, hero unlock, SECOND CIRCLE) and one forced chip row (fit = Math.max(1,…)), so it needs about 208 px. ph is capped at 182 and nothing reflows, so the chips and notes stacked up from the bottom run into the weapons stacked down from the top. view.js rounds S so that H ranges from about 225 to 375. That puts 1080p and 768p full screen (480x270, 456x256) and phones in landscape (633x293) inside the overflow band. The 240x210 and 580x216 cases need windows under 450 px tall, so they are rare, but 320x240 (a 640x480 window) also breaks badly. This is a draw-only UI change. It doesn't touch gameplay, Math.random or hero looks. The other session's uncommitted edits to gamescreen.js only add the demoChoice import and the guard in enter(), so lines 1987-2190 are untouched. Parts of the critic's write-up are taste, not defects: the panel not spreading out when there is room (the empty band at 640x360), the UNSAVED base-40 and the logo point (a separate finding). Unrelated, but seen in the same captures: the gold line prints 'Gold 900 × 2.4000000000000004' because goldParts uses r.multiplier unformatted (gamescreen.js about line 1689). It is worth its own small fix. Evidence, all under C:/Users/I/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/015cd6aa7d087c29/bb69f979-5b65-4b71-a433-ff8642911dab/scratchpad/gfx-review/hud/verify/: v1920-victory.png and v1920-death.png, made by results-cap.mjs. Better fix: The diagnosis and margins in parts (1) and (2) are right, but the proposed fix is much more than needed: a full top-down relayout with a five-stage drop order, two-column weapons, an MVP-only line, paged STATS/WEAPONS, extra section gaps and UNSAVED base-40. A smaller fix keeps the two-stack design and extends the existing rule that the rows give way before the unlocked do, so the weapons give way too. (1) Bottom margin, +2 px: - Armory/volatile line at y+ph-13. - ny = y+ph-22, and near lines at y+ph-22-(nNear-i)*10. - gy = y+ph-53-nNear*10. - need() ends with +57 instead of +55, and base = 180+…. (2) After unH and nWounds are known, compute the overflow: over = tight + unH - cap If over > 0, the weapons list gives way: - In side mode, only the part taller than the rows column counts: wExtra = max(0, nw*15 - rows*10). - In stacked mode it is nw*15. - nwShow = max(minimum, nw - ceil(over/15)). The minimum is ceil(rows*10/15) in side mode and 1 when stacked. - Then tight -= (nw - nwShow)*15. Draw the weapons sorted by damage (the MVP stays first) and slice(0, nwShow). Where some were dropped, the last shown row's name reads '+K more'. Keep the one guaranteed chip row: unlocks matter more than the sixth weapon's damage line, and that is the code's own stated priority. Notes and chips stay anchored from the bottom as now. Because the shown weapons now fit, nothing overprints, and the 'side mode chips under the weapons column' complaint goes away on its own. Leave the empty space at large sizes, the UNSAVED band and the logo scale alone, or handle them as separate items.

### hud-2 [confirmed] HUD layer order: the ultimate cut-in band is drawn under the hero's HP bar, edge markers, banners and the ult label; world labels overprint the loadout plates
- bug / code - public/js/game/hud.js:381-406 (Hud.draw: drawUltLaunch 2nd at 386; drawOrbs ult label 692/697, drawNames hero bar 1003-1012, drawIndicators 395, drawBanners 400 all after it); 703-708 (ult kill counter not in markerBand 1170-1190) [both critics: art + tech]
- Evidence: out-chrome/c1280-17-ult-cutin.png: the hero's HP bar is on the WHIRLWIND band at x622-656, y403. The 'Lucky Clover' banner (y~200) is at full brightness over the dim. The '×3' kill counter (x1120-1165, y~680) sits on the purple pointer. out-headless/h-ultcutin-240x210.png: the HP bar runs through the 'A' of SANCTUM (670-770, 715-725). The red arrow and shrine icon are at (65-170, 890-925), and the purple arrow and icon on the 'M' (1270-1370, 820-860). The 'SANCTUM' ult label is inside the band (1170-1415, 905-945): label y = H-ORB_S-15 = 147, band 103-158. art-extra/crowded240-header.png: the HP bar cuts the A and O of the plate header ('WEHPUNS').
- drawUltLaunch paints the radial dim and the cut-in band at the start of Hud.draw, so everything after it lands on the band. World-anchored labels (hero bar, elite names, boss bar, speech) are drawn after the HUD chrome, so they overprint the plates and orbs.
- Fix: Split drawUltLaunch. Keep the dim pass first, so the orbs and loadout stay bright. Move the band/pose/logo part (hud.js:828-849) to the end of Hud.draw, after drawBanners, drawArrival and drawTouch. While g.ultLaunch is set, skip drawIndicators, drawBanners and the hero bar, as is already done for g.card. Move drawNames, drawBoss, drawSpeech and drawCryptStrike before drawLoadout and drawOrbs. Register the kill-counter rect with keep() in markerBand. For the hidden hero at 240x210, see the separate loadout/camera finding.
- Reproduce sceptic (real, defect): I confirmed this in the code and in the pixels. Hud.draw (hud.js:381-406) calls drawUltLaunch second, so the dim, the band, the pose and the logo are all painted before everything else. drawOrbs (ult label and kill counter), drawNames (hero HP bar, 1003-1012), drawIndicators, drawSpeech and drawBanners all come later and land on top of the band. The band always sits at 0.49H-0.75H and the hero stands at about 0.5H, so his HP bar (feet + 3) lands on the band at every resolution: - c1280-17: a red dash at (622-656, 403) on the WHIRLWIND band. The band hides his legs but not the bar. - c1920-17 (desert): the red dash sits right over the 'A' of SANCTUM. - h-ultcutin-240x210: the bar runs through the 'A'. The red and purple edge markers sit on the band. The SANCTUM label (y=H-ORB_S-15=147) is inside the band (by=103, bh=55, so 103-158), which matches the finding's numbers. Kill counter: zoomed into c1280-17, the purple shrine marker covers the '3' of '×3'. markerBand (1170-1190) never keeps that rect, and the counter is drawn in drawOrbs before drawIndicators places markers over it. Unlike the band, this overlap can last the whole ult. World labels: drawNames and drawBoss run after drawLoadout. overHead only rejects positions off-screen, so elite names and bars and the boss bar overprint the plates. art-extra/crowded240-header shows it at 240x210. I re-rendered with the production order and with a patched order, done in memory only, in verify/sk-ult/ult-{240x210,640x360}-{prod,patched}.png. With the band drawn last, the HP bar, the markers, the ult label and a stacked banner's subtitle all go under the 0.92-alpha band, and the band reads clean. Two parts of the claim don't hold up: - 'Lucky Clover at full brightness over the dim' is not a defect. The dim is meant to leave the HUD bright (orbs, loadout, timer, LV), and the banner is HUD and sits outside the band. - 'As is already done for g.card' is wrong for two of the three items. Only drawBanners checks g.card; drawIndicators and the hero bar do not. The fix as written is partly unsound. Skipping drawBanners while g.ultLaunch is set would make a running banner blink out and come back, because fadeReal (game.js:2822-2830) keeps banner clocks running during ultLaunch and only holds hint banners. It would also hide the banners, markers and hero bar for about 0.2 s after the band has left the screen. The band's t reaches T.dur (0.6) and slides off, but g.ultLaunch lives until L.t >= 0.6, which is t = 0.8. Once the band is drawn last, those skips aren't needed at all. Severity: this is a visible layering flaw lasting about 0.6 s per ult, plus a readout that gets covered during the ult. Nothing breaks, so I rate it a defect rather than a bug. Better fix: 1. Split drawUltLaunch into drawUltDim (the radial gradient, 828-831) and drawUltBand (the band, pose and logo, 833-849). 2. Keep drawUltDim where drawUltLaunch is now. Call drawUltBand after drawBanners, drawArrival, drawSlain and the minimap, but before drawTouch, so the touch stick stays visible while the hero keeps moving. 3. Do NOT skip drawIndicators, drawBanners or the hero bar during g.ultLaunch. The 0.92-alpha band already covers whatever it overlaps, and skipping would blink running banners (their clocks keep running in fadeReal) and outlast the band by about 0.2 s. 4. Move drawBoss, drawNames and drawSpeech up so they run right after drawUltDim, before drawXp, drawLoadout, drawTop and drawOrbs. World labels then go under the HUD chrome. drawCryptStrike already clamps above belt.y and can stay where it is. 5. In drawOrbs, store the kill counter's rect, e.g. this.killBox = {x, y, w: c.width*2, h: c.height*2}. In markerBand, when g.ultRun?.kills > 0, call keep(killBox.x - 2, killBox.y - 2, killBox.w + 4, killBox.h + 4) so edge markers keep clear of '×N'.
- Intent sceptic (real, defect): The finding is real, and nothing shows the draw order was chosen on purpose. Hud.draw (public/js/game/hud.js:381-406) calls drawUltLaunch second. It went in with dfaacd1 ("The ultimate as a moment…"). That commit wants the dim under the HUD ("the hero and the HUD go on"), but nothing says the band, pose or logo should sit under world labels and markers. I found no earlier review in reviews/ that covers the cut-in layering or the kill counter, and the change touches none of the owner's settled calls. hud.js is clean in git status, so the other session's uncommitted work isn't involved. I checked the captures: - verify/herobar-band-1280.png: the hero's feet are dimmed under the 92%-alpha band, but his HP bar is drawn bright on top of it (y≈403). - verify/killcounter-marker-1280.png: the purple pointer and icon land on the "×3" kill counter. markerBand (1170-1190) never calls keep() for the counter. This one lasts the whole ultimate, not just the cut-in. - h-ultcutin-240x210.png: at a size the repo's own tools/hud-review.mjs checks, the HP bar cuts the "A" of SANCTUM, edge arrows and icons sit on the pose and the "M", and the ultLabel (y = H-ORB_S-15 ≈ 147) prints a second "SANCTUM" inside the band (103-158). The cut-in itself lasts only 0.6 s (ULT_LAUNCH.dur). So I rate it a visible defect in a showpiece moment rather than a functional bug. The kill-counter collision is the longest-lasting part. The bright "Lucky Clover" banner over the dim is not a defect: it sits above the band, and the commit intends the HUD to stay bright. The proposed fix only touches drawing. It adds no Math.random calls and changes no gameplay or hero looks. But part of it goes too far: - Skipping drawIndicators, drawBanners and the hero bar while g.ultLaunch is set isn't needed once the band is drawn last. The opaque band already covers them, and the skip would make banners and markers above the band blink out for 0.6 s for no reason. - Moving drawBoss and drawSpeech under drawLoadout/drawOrbs could hide the boss's health bar and taunt behind the plates when the boss stands low on screen. That trades an overlap for lost information. - drawCryptStrike is already clamped above belt.y-15, so moving it does nothing. Better fix: 1) Split drawUltLaunch into two parts: drawUltDim (the radial gradient, hud.js:820-826) and drawUltBand (the band, pose and logo, 827-849). Call drawUltDim where drawUltLaunch is called now. Call drawUltBand near the end of Hud.draw, after drawBanners, drawArrival and drawSlain, and before the minimap, drawTouch and drawDepartingSoul. Don't add the ultLaunch skips for indicators, banners or the hero bar: the band covers whatever sits under it. 2) In drawOrbs, store the kill counter's drawn rect (this.killRect = [x, y, c.width*2, c.height*2] while g.ultRun && run.kills > 0, otherwise null). In markerBand, add `if (this.killRect) keep(...this.killRect)` so edge markers keep clear of the counter. 3) For world labels overprinting the plates, move only drawNames (elite names and bars, thief bar, hero bar) ahead of drawLoadout. Leave drawBoss and drawSpeech where they are, or clamp the boss bar's y above belt.y as drawCryptStrike already does. Leave drawCryptStrike where it is. The hero standing behind the plates at 240x210 belongs to the separate loadout/camera finding.

### hud-3 [confirmed] Pixel art at fractional scales: ultimate cut-in pose, signature-evolution pose, logo-font titles, pop-in tweens
- defect / code - public/js/game/hud.js:845-848 (ps=(bh-6)/pf.height; ns=min(1,W*0.62/name.width)), 881 (card pop), 1307-1311 (banner s=2*pop); public/js/ui/gamescreen.js:1352-1357 (ceremony s=4*pop, evolved name ls), 1365 (signature ps=min(2,64/h)), 1430-1440 (evolutionLogo kk), 1986-1989 (results logo s); public/js/ui/trophy.js:48-50 [both critics: art + tech]
- Evidence: Measured with the real sprites (art-extra/poseh.mjs, logow.mjs, logoh.mjs; tech-extra/sizes.mjs). Cut-in pose: Knight 2.667 at 1280x720, 1.939 at 1080p, 1.485 at 240x210; Mage 1.600 at 1080p; 19 of 20 heroes non-integer at 1080p; 633x293 Knight 2.12, only the Necromancer is integer. raw/c1280-17: pose pixel runs of 4 and 6 device px mixed (tech-extra/cutin-pose-1280.png; art-extra/cutin-pose-480.png, Paladin). Signature pose: 1.600-1.939 for 15 of 20 heroes. Ult names: 'ARMY OF THE DEAD' (360 px) 0.827 at 1080p, 0.999 at 580 wide; 0.41-0.89 for every hero but the Witch Hunter at 240. 'YOU HAVE FALLEN' (344 px) 0.640 at 240, 0.87 at 320/640x480 (uneven Y/O/U strokes, tech-extra/fallen-logo-320.png). 'DAWN BREAKS' 0.859 at 240. EVOLUTION! (216 px) 0.556 at 240, 0.741 at 320. 'Heartwood Convergence' (496) and 'Legion of the Damned' (448) shrunk even at 480 wide. Pops tween 3.2→2, 4.8→3, 6→4.
- With nearest-neighbour sampling, a non-integer scale duplicates some pixel columns and rows and drops others. The plumed-Knight-standard heroes and the logo font lose their 1-px edges exactly in the showcase moments: every ultimate, evolution, death and victory title.
- Fix: Add a helper ik = (target,size) => Math.max(1, Math.floor(target/size)). Use it for the cut-in pose (centre the leftover space in the band) and the signature pose. Add fitLogo(text, cols, maxW): the logo at x1 if it fits, else textCanvas(text,…,'bold') at x2 or x1, or wrap onto two lines, never a fractional drawImage. Use it in hud.js:847, gamescreen.js:1354-1357, 1430-1434, 1986-1989 and trophy.js:48-50. For pops, step through integer scales (e.g. x3 for 0.06 s / 2 frames, then x2).
- Reproduce sceptic (real, defect): I confirmed this in the code, in measurements and in pixels. The HUD draws into the low-res W×H canvas (view.js), which presentUI then blows up by a whole number S. So any fractional drawImage inside the HUD repeats some art columns and rows and drops others. Cut-in pose (hud.js:845-846): ps=(bh-6)/pf.height. My own run with the real SPR poses (verify/scales.mjs) gives these results. - 1280x720 (S2, 640x360): Knight 2.667, Mage 2.200, Ea-nasir 1.913. 0 of 22 heroes get a whole-number scale. - 1920x1080 (480x270): Knight 1.939, Mage 1.600. Only the four 32-px-tall recruits land on 2.000. - 1366x768 and 2560x1440: 0 of 22. The raw Chrome capture raw/c1280-17-ult-cutin.png, which comes from the real launchUlt path and is no capture artefact, has horizontal pixel runs of 6:1539 and 4:812 in the pose box. So art pixels come out 3 or 2 UI px wide, mixed. The 4x crop (verify/cutin1280-crop.png) shows uneven eye, armour and boot blocks. Signature pose (gamescreen.js:1365): ps=min(2,64/h) gives 1.39-1.94 for most heroes. verify/compare.png shows the Mage at 1.6 next to 2.0, and his face and staff pixels are clearly uneven. Logo titles: ns/ls/s=min(1,…) shrinks the 4x logo font by a fraction. - "YOU HAVE FALLEN" (344 px): 0.872 at W=320, 0.640 at W=240. verify/logo-zoom.png shows ragged Y/O shapes and a dotted shadow. - "ARMY OF THE DEAD" (360 px): 0.827 at 480 wide, 0.785 at 456. - Evolution names: "Heartwood Convergence" 0.927 at 480. "High Quality Copper (not for sale)" is 744 px, so it shrinks at every size (0.618 at 480, 0.833 at 640). Pop tweens (hud.js:881, 1307-1311, gamescreen.js:1352) do run through fractional scales, but only for 0.12-0.3 s while moving, so that part is polish. The critic's numbers match mine apart from small hero-count differences. Earlier reviews never settled this point, and the codebase already aims for whole-number scales elsewhere (drawScaled, the overlay floor in presentUI). Severity: this is a defect, not a bug. It hits every ultimate cut-in at every common desktop size, plus the evolution and death showcases. File status: hud.js and trophy.js are clean. gamescreen.js has another session's uncommitted edits, but only at lines 33 and 42 (import and constructor), not at the cited lines. The proposed fix is not sound, because of the Math.floor. - At 1080p, floor(64/h) drops 16 of 22 heroes (every pose taller than 32 px) from about 1.9x to 1x. The cut-in Knight would shrink to 33 px in a 70-px band while the Ranger and Berserker stay at 60 px. That is half-size, mismatched poses. - The same happens to the signature pose: floor(min(2,64/h)) gives 1x for 16 of 22 heroes. - Swapping the logo for textCanvas 'bold' changes the showcase typeface. Better fix: 1. Cut-in pose: use k = Math.max(1, Math.round((bh - 6) / pf.height)) instead of floor. Anchor the pose to the band's bottom, x at W*0.18 - pf.width*k/2 and y = by + bh - 3 - pf.height*k. Let it break out of the band's top (a usual cut-in look), or grow bh to pf.height*k + 6. With round, every hero gets 2x at 1080p and 1366x768 except Ea-nasir (1x at 1366, 1.326 rounds down), and 3x at 1280x720 except the Mage, Druid, Arcanist, Thornkeeper and Ea-nasir, who get 2x.    Alternative with no layout change: pass the pose to view.overlay(pf, x + off, y, w, h, {fill: null}). presentUI already draws overlays at a whole number of device pixels per art pixel, so the size stays within one device pixel of today. Note that overlays sit above the whole UI and skip ctx alpha and transforms. 2. Signature pose: draw at exactly 2x (Math.round(min(2, 64/h)) gives 2 for every pose 43 px tall or less). Raise it a few px so the 66-80 px tall poses clear the evolved name logo at cy+40. 3. Titles: add a fitLogo(text, cols, maxW). It returns the normal logo at 1x when it fits. Otherwise it returns the same logo built with one scale2x pass instead of two: give logo() a times/size parameter, which keeps the bevel, colour bands and outline at half size. Next it tries two lines, and only last resort scales down. Never a fractional drawImage. Use it at hud.js:847, gamescreen.js:1354-1357, 1430-1434, 1986-1989 and trophy.js:48-50. 4. Pop tweens: optional polish. Round the pop scale to whole steps (Math.round(big*pop), so 3 then 2) or leave as is; it is a 0.12-0.3 s moving tween. Separately clamp the boss card's resting s2 to a whole number (Math.max(1, Math.floor(...)) with a smaller font fallback).
- Intent sceptic (real, defect): Confirmed with the production code and sprites (my re-run of the critic's size script, plus verify/fractional/pose-compare.mjs, which renders pose-1080-frac-vs-int.png and army-logo-1080-083-vs-1.png). **Why the scales come out fractional.** The HUD is drawn on a W×H canvas, and presentUI blows it up by a whole-number S. Any fractional drawImage on that canvas therefore mixes pixel sizes on screen. **Cut-in pose (hud.js:845-846).** The scale is ps = (bh-6)/pf.height, and it is fractional at every common size: - 1920x1080 (W=480): Knight 1.94, Mage 1.60, Ea-nasir 1.39. - 1280x720: Knight 2.67, so runs of 2 and 3 UI px (4 and 6 device px). - A 1920x960 window (W=640): Knight 2.33, Mage 1.93. At 1.6, 16 of the Mage's 40 rows come out half-height. These are the 1-px dark edges the plumed-Knight style depends on, shown big in the ultimate's showcase. **Signature pose (gamescreen.js:1365).** min(2, 64/h) is fractional for most heroes. **Ultimate name at 1080p.** 'ARMY OF THE DEAD' is drawn at 0.827. The render shows uneven M, Y and E strokes and lost bevel pixels. **Other logos.** 'YOU HAVE FALLEN', 'DAWN BREAKS', EVOLUTION! and the TROPHY title only shrink on screens under about 360 UI px wide (portrait phones, 640x480), so they matter less. **Not deliberate or settled.** - The lines came in with dfaacd1 and 1c84500, and no comment or commit message justifies a fractional scale. - The code elsewhere goes out of its way to keep whole-number scales: drawScaled is commented "crisp: a text canvas drawn k times its size", presentUI uses "whole multiples" of an image's size, and chestItemScale returns 1 or 2. - No review in reviews/ discusses it. review-followups-2026-11-15 even rejects a change because fractional sampling lost quality. - It touches none of the owner's settled calls. **Pops.** The banner, card and icon pops are transient 0.1-0.3 s tweens. Making those integer is taste-level and low value. **Other session's file.** gamescreen.js has that session's uncommitted edits, but only 3 lines (an import and demo mode, around lines 33 and 42). The target lines are untouched. hud.js, trophy.js and ui.js are clean. Better fix: **Problem with the proposed fix.** The helper floor(target/size) halves the hero exactly where it is seen most. At 1920x1080 the cut-in scale becomes 1 for 8 of 10 heroes (Knight 1.94, Paladin 1.88, Mage 1.60, Necro 1.83, Shadow 1.88, Druid 1.68, Witch Hunter 1.94, Ea-nasir 1.39). The signature pose drops to 1x for most heroes too, so the pose ends up 33 px tall in a 70 px band, a clear regression. The textCanvas-bold fallback also drops the logo's Scale2x/bevel look. **Smaller and on-style:** 1. Cut-in, hud.js:845-846:    - k = Math.max(1, Math.round((bh-6)/pf.height)).    - Grow the band to fit: bh = Math.max(Math.round(H*0.26), k*pf.height + 6), computed before by.    - Draw the pose with whole k, centred at W*0.18.    - Knight at 1080p becomes 2x (66 px, band 72); Mage 2x (80 px, band 86). Both fit easily. 2. Signature pose, gamescreen.js:1365: ps = Math.max(1, Math.round(64/pf.height)). Mage at 2x spans cy±40, which still clears 'SIGNATURE' horizontally and only touches the logo at cy+40. Ea-nasir 1x. 3. Logos: one helper fitLogo(text, cols, maxW).    - Use the 4x logo if it fits.    - Else the same logo built with ONE Scale2x pass. This is a small optional param in ui.js logo(), times 1 instead of 2 for the 'normal' font; ui.js has no WIP.    - Else textCanvas bold at the largest whole number that fits.    - Never a fractional drawImage.    - Apply it at hud.js:847-848, gamescreen.js:1354-1357, 1430-1434 and 1986-1989, and trophy.js:48-50. 4. Leave the pops as they are, or do them last. Integer stepping there is taste-level. Draw-only: no gameplay, no Math.random, and the hero looks stay as picked. gamescreen.js has another session's uncommitted 3-line demo change, away from these lines, so stage hunks by name.

### hud-4 [confirmed] Banners have no fit-to-width and stack blindly over the boss's name and bar and into the loadout
- bug / code - public/js/game/hud.js:1294-1316 (drawBanners: s=2*pop always, y from max(H*0.22, top+4), +24/34 per banner with no limit); 763-812 (drawBoss: bar at the boss's head, drawn earlier, unaware of banners) [both critics: art + tech]
- Evidence: out-headless/h-ultreceipt-240x210.png: the receipt 'CATACLYSM · 187 slain · 48.2K damage' is a 176-px canvas drawn at x2 (352 px), clipped to 'CLYSM · 187 slain · 48.2K d'. It is also wider than a 320-px screen. h-crowded-240x210.png: 'OSSARIAN' and its bar (x920-1280, y250-340) are run through by 'A swarm of bats!', and 'Damage +60% for 30s' ends at y≈114 with the WEAPONS plate at 117. h-crowded-320x240 (x700-970, y270-345): the boss name is fully hidden, with the bar under 'from the north-west · 2 of 4'. h-crowded-580x216 (x640-760, y100-135): same. The pop 1.6→1 is non-integer for the first 12% of each banner.
- Titles are always drawn at x2 and the sub-lines are never cut to fit. The stack ignores the boss bar, belt.y and the other banners, and it is painted after drawBoss. A boss arriving while a swarm, shrine or receipt banner is up is common late at night, and then both texts become unreadable.
- Fix: Per banner: s = c.width*2 <= W-8 ? 2 : 1 (the drawSlain rule, hud.js:1286). If it is still too wide, fitText(title, W-8); always fitText(sub, W-8). Use integer pop steps. Measure the stack first. If it hits the boss bar's rect (barPoint plus the name 11 px above), move the block below the bar (bar.y+10), or above the name. Alternatively, record the stack rect and have drawBoss avoid it, or draw drawBoss after the banners. Clamp the block above belt.y-2 (stop when y+24 > belt.y-4) by dropping sub-lines first, then the oldest banner, or let extra banners wait. Hold banners during g.ultLaunch.
- Reproduce sceptic (real, defect): Confirmed in pixels and in code. hud.js and game.js are not in the other session's uncommitted files. (1) Width. drawBanners (hud.js:1310) always draws the title at s = 2*pop and never fits the sub-line. I measured textCanvas widths at x2: - Cataclysm receipt: 352 px - Whirlwind receipt: 340 px - Arrow Storm receipt: 352 px - Army of the Dead receipt: 416 px - 'The copper contract is complete': 288 px - sub 'Copper discs attack automatically...': 276 px The game really picks widths this narrow. view.js:57 sets S=round(min/300), so a 1024x768 window gives 342x256, iPad landscape about 410x308, iPad portrait about 308 wide, phone portrait about 270, and a 640x480 window 320. I re-rendered with production code: verify/v-receipt-boss-342x256.png shows 'Y OF THE DEAD · 312 slain · 120K dam', and verify/v-receipt-boss-410x308.png has both ends clipped. h-ultreceipt-240x210.png shows 'CLYSM · 187 slain · 48.2K d' exactly as cited. During the pop, s reaches 3.2, so even a 480-wide screen overflows briefly. (2) Overlap. drawBoss runs at hud.js:391 and drawBanners at :400, so banners are painted over the bar. The bar follows the boss's head (overHead, AT.y-6), and that head sits in the banner band (0.22H to about 0.22H+68) whenever a boss stands near the hero. Ult receipts usually fire during boss fights, and spawner events never check for a boss. What the images show: - h-crowded-240x210: 'A swarm of bats!' runs through the Ossarian bar. - h-crowded-320x240: the name is fully hidden and the bar sits under the sub-line. - h-crowded-580x216: same. - My 410x308 render: the GRIMGUT name is hidden under the receipt and the bar under BEST. Overstated parts: - 'no limit' is wrong: game.js:74 BANNERS_MOST=2 caps the stack at 2 banners, at most 68 px. - 'into the loadout' is not shown: at 240x210 the second sub-line ends at about y108 and the WEAPONS plate starts at about y117, so they don't touch. - Holding banners during ultLaunch has no evidence here. - The non-integer pop lasts only 0.18-0.4 s, so it is polish at most. The fix is right on width but not minimal elsewhere. Its main suggestion is to move the whole banner block each frame below or above the bar. The bar tracks a walking boss, so the banners would ride up and down with it. The belt clamp, dropping the oldest banner and the ult hold all answer things that weren't shown. Better fix: Width (hud.js drawBanners): - Per banner, use `const s0 = c.width * 2 <= W - 8 ? 2 : 1`. This is the drawSlain rule (hud.js:1286). - Make the pop integer: `s = u < 0.06 && c.width * 3 <= W - 8 ? 3 : s0`, or skip the pop when s0 is 1. - Draw the sub as `fitText(b.sub, W - 8)`. Move gamescreen.js's local fitText (line 1465) into engine/font.js and import it in both places. - Cleaner option for the receipt (game.js:2507), the owner's call since ultimate.js:7 describes it as one line: banner(name.toUpperCase(), `${kills} slain · ${compact(dmg)} damage${best ? ' · BEST' : ''}`, ...). The title stays at x2 on every screen; ARMY OF THE DEAD is about 160 px. Overlap: leave the banners still and have the bar give way. - In drawBanners, record the block it drew: `this.bannerBand = { y0: start - 4, y1: y }`, or null when nothing is drawn. - Draw the banners before drawBoss, or use last frame's band. - In drawBoss, after computing y, check whether the name-and-bar rect [y-12, y+8] meets the band. If it does, snap y to the nearer free side, band.y1 + 12 or band.y0 - 9, and still clamp it to the screen. - The bar leaves the head only while a banner is up, and nothing jitters because the band only changes when banners come and go. No belt clamp or ult hold is needed, since BANNERS_MOST=2 already bounds the stack.
- Intent sceptic (real, defect): This is a real problem. Nothing in the reviews/ folder settles it, it goes against none of the owner's calls, and git history shows no deliberate choice behind it. The H*0.22 start comes from db3987d and only keeps the banners below the top lines. I checked it at real device sizes, using renders made with the production Game and HUD under hud/verify/banners/. (1) Width. Every ultimate receipt is 340-432 px wide at x2: CATACLYSM 352, ARMY OF THE DEAD 432. "The copper contract is complete" is 288. Measured with textCanvas in verify/banners/widths.mjs. - iPhone portrait (390x844@3, so S=4 and W=293): the receipt is clipped to "TACLYSM · 312 slain · 148K dama" (c-phone-293.png). - iPad landscape (1024x768@2, so W=410): the ARMY OF THE DEAD receipt touches both edges (v-ipad-410.png). - The codebase already has the fix pattern in drawSlain (hud.js:1286), and the loadout already plans for narrow widths (aboveOrbs). (2) Boss bar. At plain 1920x1080 (480x270), the Bone King's bar sits beside the hero, and "A swarm of bats!" plus its sub-line completely hide the name OSSARIAN (c-crowded-480.png, left). A receipt right after an ultimate on a boss, or a swarm warning during a boss fight, makes this common. Parts of the critic's write-up are wrong or not worth doing: - "No limit" is false. BANNERS_MOST = 2 (game.js:74) caps the stack at 68 px. - The loadout collision only shows in a 240x210 window at S=1. That is not a real device: view.js sets S so the short side is about 225-375, so a narrow screen is always tall. - Integer pop steps are taste: the 0.3 s pop-in is deliberate. - Holding banners during the ultimate's cut-in is out of scope. The suggested boss fix doesn't hold up either: - Moving the up-to-68 px banner block below the bar puts it on the hero. - Above the name there is only about 41 px (top lines end at 18, the stack starts at 59). - Either way the block would jump about as the boss walks. - Drawing drawBoss after the banners just swaps which text is lost. I rendered it: OSSARIAN then cuts through "bats!" (c-crowded-480.png, right). The width part of the fix is sound and small. Note that fitText lives in gamescreen.js, which is part of the other session's uncommitted work. hud.js itself is not in that work. Better fix: Change hud.js only. No gameplay change and no Math.random calls. (a) In drawBanners, size each banner by drawSlain's rule and fit its sub-line: `const s = (c.width * 2 <= W - 8 ? 2 : 1) * pop;` and `drawText(ctx, fitText(b.sub, W - 8), ...)` Use a local 4-line fitText copied from gamescreen.js:1465, or move it into engine/font.js. Don't import it from gamescreen.js: that file is in the other session's uncommitted work. With this, receipts drop to x1 (176-216 px) on phones and the iPad, which fits any W of 225 or more. (b) Keep the banners where they are and move the boss bar out of their way: - At the end of drawBanners, record the drawn stack's span: `this.bannerSpan = drew ? { top: y0 - 1, bottom: y } : null`. - In drawBoss, after computing y: if a span exists and the bar-plus-name rect [y - 11, y + 7] overlaps it, set `y = Math.max(this.topLines() + 16, span.top - 9)`. This lifts the name and bar into the free band between the clock row and the banners. At 480x270 that band runs from 18 to 59, room enough for the 18 px they need. - Using last frame's span gives a one-frame lag nobody will see. The bar stays over the foe's head whenever there is no clash. Leave out the loadout clamp (BANNERS_MOST = 2 already caps the stack), the integer pop and the hold during the ultimate's cut-in.

### hud-5 [confirmed] The gold formula prints the raw float multiplier ('× 2.4000000000000004'), and '× 1' on Normal
- bug / code - public/js/ui/gamescreen.js:1689-1692 (goldParts: part.mult = ` × ${m}` and the `own ? '' : ` × ${m}`` fallback); the float comes from public/js/shared/profile.js:872 (DIFF_GOLD × NIGHTMARE_GOLD × oathGold); profile.js has uncommitted changes at other lines [both critics: art + tech]
- Evidence: out-chrome/c1280-33-victory.png at (360-920, y~535): 'Gold 900 × 2.4000000000000004 + 1080 wage + 800 bonus' (Elite 1.5 × oathGold(6) 1.6). Also in c320-33 (y~680) and c240-33, where the float forces the shortest form or fitText truncation. out-chrome/c1280-23-death-recap.png y~369: 'Collected 70 × 1 + 43 wage for 01:25'.
- The multiplier is interpolated raw. A product of floats shows 16-digit noise and costs width on small screens. A multiplier of 1 is still printed as a factor.
- Fix: Display only: const fmtMult = m => `× ${+m.toFixed(2)}`, used in both places, and omit the factor when Math.abs(m-1) < 1e-9. The receipt sanitizer keeps its exact float. Optionally round the stored multiplier to 2 decimals where the reward is computed, so the line always adds up.
- Reproduce sceptic (real, bug): The bug is real, in both the pixels and the code. In c1280-33-victory.png at y~535 the line reads "Gold 900 × 2.4000000000000004 + 1080 wage + 800 bonus". c320-33 shows the same line. The capture did nothing the game wouldn't do: chrome-capture.mjs starts the run normally with diff 'hard' and oaths iron+glass+kings (heat 6). The code matches. gamescreen.js:1691-1692 (goldParts) puts r.multiplier into the text raw: `part.mult = \` × ${m}\`` and the `own ? '' : \` × ${m}\`` fallback. profile.js:872 computes it as DIFF_GOLD × NIGHTMARE_GOLD × oathGold(h), where oathGold = 1 + 0.1h. Many ordinary products are float noise: - 1.5 × 1.6 = 2.4000000000000004 - 0.75 × 1.1 = 0.8250000000000001 - 1 × 1.7 = 1.7000000000000002 - 1.5 × 1.4 = 2.0999999999999996 I ran the production goldSteps/goldParts/runReward/textWidth in node (verify/goldline.mjs). The raw float makes the line 280 px wide, which pushes it to the shortest form ("Gold … bonus") even at w=323. With "× 2.4" the full form fits: "Collected 900 × 2.4 + 1080 wage for 15:00 + 800 victory bonus" (293 px). At w<280 the raw line goes through fitText truncation, as the finding says. The "× 1" part is misdescribed. "Collected 70 × 1 + 43 wage" (c1280-23) is Veteran (id medium, rate 1) with no oaths, not Normal. Normal (easy) shows "× 0.75". The comment at gamescreen.js:1669-1674 says a rate of 1 or under is shown beside the gold from the start on purpose. So dropping "× 1" is optional taste, not part of the bug. The proposed fix has two problems: 1. toFixed(2) is too few digits. Twelve reachable multipliers need three decimals: 0.825, 0.975, 1.125, 1.275, 1.425, 1.575, 2.475, 2.925, 3.375, 3.825, 4.275, 4.725. With two digits the line would show "900 × 0.83" next to a collected 743, where 900 × 0.83 = 747, so it no longer adds up. 2. The optional step of rounding the stored multiplier is harmful. It changes the gold actually paid (runReward). It also breaks sanitizeReceipt (profile.js:901), which looks the stored multiplier up in RECEIPT_MULTIPLIERS within 1e-9. A value of 0.83 matches nothing, so the receipt quietly falls back to × 1. Neither cited spot is in the other session's uncommitted hunks. gamescreen.js and profile.js have unrelated changes in progress elsewhere in the files. Better fix: Change the display only, in public/js/ui/gamescreen.js goldParts. Add `const fmtMult = (m) => \` × ${+m.toFixed(3)}\`;`. Every multiplier is a product of {0.75, 1, 1.5, 2.25} and (1 + 0.1h), so it has at most three decimals, and this prints it exactly. Use it in both places: `part.mult = fmtMult(m)` and `${own ? '' : fmtMult(m)}`. Leave r.multiplier and runReward untouched, because the stored float sets what is paid and is what sanitizeReceipt matches. Hiding "× 1" (Veteran, no oaths) is optional, the owner's call; the code comment shows that rate is printed on purpose. The other session has uncommitted work in progress in gamescreen.js elsewhere in the file, so stage only this hunk.
- Intent sceptic (real, bug): I confirmed this on the capture. The crop at C:/Users/I/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/015cd6aa7d087c29/bb69f979-5b65-4b71-a433-ff8642911dab/scratchpad/gfx-review/hud/verify/gold-formula-1280.png shows 'Gold 900 × 2.4000000000000004 + 1080 wage + 800 bonus'. The extra width pushes the line down to its shortest form ('Gold', 'bonus') even at 1280 px. Cause: goldParts (public/js/ui/gamescreen.js:1689-1692) prints r.multiplier as it is. The multiplier is DIFF_GOLD × NIGHTMARE_GOLD × oathGold(heat), with oathGold = 1 + 0.1*heat (oaths shipped 2026-11-21), so it picks up floating-point noise. When I enumerated the combinations, many common ones are noisy: Normal heat 1 → 0.8250000000000001, heat 2 → 0.8999999999999999; Elite heat 1 → 1.6500000000000001, heat 4 → 2.0999999999999996; Veteran heat 7 → 1.7000000000000002. This is a new regression from the oaths work, not a settled or deliberate choice. Commit 341fa1f (C11) only chose to stamp rates above ×1 and to show Normal's ×0.75 from the start. The '× 1' half is not a defect. On Veteran with no oaths the code deliberately prints the rate whenever it has no stamp of its own, consistent with '× 0.75' on Normal. Dropping it is taste. Neither gamescreen.js hunk that the other session has not committed touches these lines (they are at lines 33 and 42). No test matches the formula text. The proposed fix is not quite right. toFixed(2) loses precision: possible rates sit on a 0.075 grid, so 0.825, 2.925 and 4.275 would show as 0.83, 2.93 and 4.28 (or 4.27), and the formula would no longer match the gold collected. Rounding the stored multiplier inside runReward changes the economy code (Math.round on values ending in .5) and should be skipped. Better fix: Change the display only, in goldParts. Add `const mt = \` × ${+m.toFixed(3)}\`;` and use it in both places: `part.mult = mt` and `own ? '' : mt`. Three decimals is exact for every rate the game can produce, since d*(1+0.1h) with d in {0.75, 1, 1.5, 2.25} has at most 3 decimals, and the unary plus drops trailing zeros (2.4, 1.65, 0.825). Keep '× 1' as it is (it matches how ×0.75 is shown); omitting it is optional taste. Leave runReward, the stored multiplier and the receipt sanitizer untouched.

### hud-7 [confirmed] Compact (stacked) level-up cards and merchant/Trophy/Altar cards spill icons, pips, text and prices out of their frames; large merchant cards are half empty and the price is buried in prose
- bug / code - public/js/ui/gamescreen.js:721 (vertical h), 839-840 (Scale2x 32-px icon at y+5, pips at fixed y+41); public/js/ui/trophy.js:56-76 (vertical h≈26-28, 2x icon at y+8, up to 3 description lines from y+18; horizontal h=min(140,…), description left-aligned at x+6), 88-89 (TAKE NONE) [both critics: art + tech]
- Evidence: out-chrome/c240-04-levelup-ready.png: card h≈32, but the icon reaches y+37 and the pips sit at y+41. The bow icon crosses card 2's frame, and the Lodestone icon and pips land on the footer and HP orb (60-180, 620-750). c240-10-merchant.png: ware h = min(64, floor((210-58-24)/4)-4) = 28 vs content to y+45. 'gold.' wraps below Whetstone (y~515), 'Relic Polish' sits on the border, 'Fortune Cards / 59 gold' spills over TAKE NONE (y~760-800), and the icons spill below the cards (x80-180). c1280-10-merchant.png: the 125x135 cards keep content in the top ~70 px. The name is centred but the description is left-aligned, and the price appears only inside the sentence: 'Heal 50% of your health. 47 gold.'
- The vertical layouts shrink the card height to fit the screen, but the contents keep fixed offsets sized for 64-72 px cards. The horizontal merchant layout uses a fixed height regardless of content. The merchant typography and alignment do not follow the level-up cards it copies, and the price has no visual weight.
- Fix: Size the contents from h. When h < 44, use a 1x 16-px icon, vertically centred (Scale2x or x2 only when h >= 44). Put the pips at min(y+41, y+h-6) or to the right of the name, and use lines = floor((h-18)/9). Merchant: give the price its own foot line (h_coin icon plus the number, #fee761, or #e43b44 when unaffordable) and remove it from the prose. Size the cards to content (h = 46+lines*9+14) and centre the description. In the vertical layout, when there is room for fewer than 2 lines, show the name plus a right-aligned price ('47 g').
- Reproduce sceptic (real, defect): The finding is real, and I confirmed it in the code, in the cited captures and in new captures at sizes that are not contrived. **Code.** In public/js/ui/gamescreen.js, line 721 sets the stacked card height: h = min(72, floor((H-top-foot)/n)-4), with top = 68 and foot = 36. Lines 839-840 still draw a 32 px Scale2x icon at y+5 and the pips at a fixed y+41, so any card shorter than about 47 px overflows. The description itself does not spill, because room = floor((h-26)/9) already clamps it. On very short cards that clamp hides the description entirely, so only names show. In public/js/ui/trophy.js (vertical layout, lines 60-68), h = min(64, floor((H-y0-24)/n)-4) with y0 = 62. The 2x icon runs from y+8 to y+40, and slice(0,3) draws up to three lines with no check against h. The horizontal layout uses h = min(140, H-y0-foot) whatever the content, and draws the description left-aligned at x+6 under a centred name. In game.js cardInfo (line 1204), the price is appended to the prose as ' {47} gold.'. It is highlighted in the same #feae34 as every other number, so it stands out no more than "50%" does. **Cited captures.** c240-04 and c240-10 match the description: the bow and Lodestone icons cross the frames, card 3's pips land on the HP orb and footer, "gold." wraps out under Whetstone, and "59 gold" runs into TAKE NONE. c1280-10 and c1920-10 show 4-line-tall content in the top half of cards about 135 px tall, with a centred name over a left-aligned description. The 240x210 viewport was set through Playwright on the real game, so this is not a capture artefact. It is, though, an extreme window size. **Reach.** I recomputed view.js S/W/H for realistic windows. Portrait phones (W 250-295, H 369-633) are fine: their cards are 53-72 px tall. The stacked layout only overflows when W < 300 and H is short: - level-up with 3 cards: H < 254 - LUCKY level-up with 4 cards: H < 304 - 5 fillers (stacked whenever W < 360): H < 332 - merchant with 4 wares: H < 282 That means small or near-square desktop windows. I captured two in Chrome (images in verify/cards/): - **1100x1100 window (275x275 art px):** LUCKY cards are 38 px tall. Every card's pips fall into the gap below it, the sword and belt icons cross the bottom frame, and card 4's pips sit on the HP orb (lu4-1100x1100.png). - **500x500 window (250x250 art px):** level-up cards are 32 px tall and the icons spill (lu4-500x500.png). Merchant cards are 37 px tall, and Fortune Cards' "gold." wraps below the card while its icon overflows (merchant-500x500.png). **Severity.** I'd rate it a defect, not a bug. The cards still work, the breakage is visible only in small, squarish windows, and the half-empty merchant cards and alignment points are polish. **Proposed fix.** Not sound as written. It bundles the overflow fix with a merchant restyle. Its rule for stacked cards with room for fewer than 2 lines ("name plus price only") would drop the one description line that still fits. On Whetstone and Relic Polish that line names which weapon or relic rises and to what level, which is what the player needs to decide. **Ownership.** gamescreen.js has another session's uncommitted 3-line change, though not in this region. trophy.js and game.js are clean. Better fix: Change only the stacked branches and keep each description: (1) Level-up, gamescreen.js lines 838-840: - Set `const big = h >= 47`. - Draw `big ? smoothScaled(icon, 1) : icon.c` at x+6 (+8 when small), centred vertically: `y + lift + (big ? 5 : Math.max(3, (h - 22) >> 1))`. - Draw the pips at `Math.min(y + lift + 41, y + lift + h - 6)`, centred under the icon (x+22 when big, x+14 when small). - The description lines are already clamped by room, so leave them. (2) Trophy, merchant and altar, trophy.js vertical branch: - Use the 2x icon only when h >= 46, otherwise 1x centred at y + ((h - 16) >> 1). - Clamp the text with `.slice(0, Math.max(1, Math.floor((h - 18) / 9)))`. - Optional: wrap it at w-56 only after taking the price out (see 3). (3) Price: - Have Game.cardInfo return `price` separately instead of appending ' {N} gold.' to desc. - Draw the price as h_coin plus the number in #fee761, or #e43b44 when info.disabled. - Stacked: put it right-aligned on the name line. That frees a line, and the description is never dropped. - Side by side: put it on its own centred line at the card foot. (4) Horizontal merchant cards: - Draw the description centred (`x + w/2, align: 'center'`), as the level-up cards do. - Set h = min(140, H - y0 - foot, 60 + maxLines*9 + 14), where maxLines is the largest wrapped line count over the cards, so the cards stop being half empty. Optional, for the root cause on short windows: scale the title logo by H as well as W (for example `s = Math.min(1, (W - 40) / L.width, (H - 200) / 60)`) so short screens give the stacked cards about 15 more px.
- Intent sceptic (real, defect): The finding is real. No settled call, comment, commit or earlier review covers it. **Origin.** trophy.js arrived only on 2026-11-21, in 6a674bf (G7 trophy) and 79c35c4 (G6 merchant). The stacked level-up layout says it is "for a phone held upright". On portrait phones h is 72 (level-up) or 64 (wares), so nothing spills there. The compact case was never intended. **How often it shows.** The critic's 240x210 capture is an extreme case: a tiny window at S=1. I worked out S from view.js for real viewports (verify/cards-sk/cap.mjs). The 16:9 desktops (640x360, 480x270, 456x256) and phones held either way never reach h<44. It does reach h<44 in near-square or small 4:3 windows: - **850x850 window (284x284, S=3), four cards:** the cards are 41 px tall. Each card's pips sit in the gap outside its frame, and the last card's pips are on the HP orb. Descriptions are cut to one line. See verify/cards-sk/sq850-levelup.png. - **640x480 window with a maxed build (five fillers, `n>4 && W<360`):** the cards are 27 px tall. Each 32-px icon spills about 10 px into the next card. The descriptions vanish completely, so the heal % and the gold amount are not shown. See verify/cards-sk/w640x480-fillers-levelup.png. - **Trophy/merchant stacked cards:** these hit the same problem when h<48 (2x icon down to y+40, three lines down to y+45). **Merchant and trophy at the most common size.** At 640x360 (1280x720 or 1920x1080 desktop) and on a landscape phone, the cards are a fixed 140 px tall with content in the top ~75 px. The name is centred while the description is left-aligned at x+6. That breaks from the level-up cards, which centre everything. The price exists only as "{47} gold." at the end of the sentence. It is highlighted orange but has no place of its own. This part is polish, not a bug. **Severity.** I rate it a defect rather than a bug: the visible breakage needs uncommon window shapes, and the common shapes only show the half-empty polish issue. **The proposed fix** is draw-only: - no gameplay change; - no `Math.random` calls; - no hero art touched. Moving the price out of the prose means `cardInfo` returns a `price` field. The tests only assert that name/desc are truthy and that `disabled` is set (test/altar.test.js:50, test/stall.test.js:56), so this is safe. **Corrections to the critic's fix:** - Its content-height formula `46+lines*9+14` is wrong. The description starts at y+60, so the height is 60 + lines×9 + 6, plus 12 for a price line. - gamescreen.js has another session's uncommitted edits (demo-mode import and `demoChoice`). The fix must stage only its own hunks. trophy.js and game.js are clean. Better fix: Draw-only changes: 1. **gamescreen.js, stacked level-up branch (~line 839).**    - Set `const tiny = h < 44`.    - Draw `tiny ? icon.c : smoothScaled(icon, 1)`. When tiny, put it at x+14, centred vertically at `y + lift + Math.max(3, Math.round((h - 16) / 2) - 2)`; otherwise keep x+6, y+5.    - Draw the pips at `y + lift + Math.min(41, h - 6)`.    - Set `room = Math.min(gold || info.hint ? 3 : 4, Math.floor((h - 18) / 9))`. 2. **trophy.js, stacked layout.**    - Apply the same tiny rule: 1x icon when h < 44, otherwise 2x.    - Show `Math.min(3, Math.floor((h - 18) / 9))` description lines.    - When no line fits, right-align the price beside the name. 3. **trophy.js, side-by-side layout.**    - Before the loop, wrap every card's description and take `maxLines`.    - Set `h = Math.min(140, H - y0 - foot, 66 + maxLines * 9 + (anyPrice ? 12 : 0))`.    - Centre the description lines at `x + w / 2` with `align: 'center'`, like the level-up cards. 4. **Price.** Have `Game.cardInfo` 'buy' return `price: c.price` and leave the " {N} gold." text out of `desc`. Draw the price on its own foot line:    - the `SPR.h_coin` icon plus the number in `#fee761`;    - the number in `RED`/`#e43b44` when `disabled`.

### hud-8 [confirmed] In-run overlays put their footer, buttons and map on the live, dimmed WEAPONS/POWERS plates
- defect / code - public/js/ui/gamescreen.js:184 (hud.draw under every overlay), 714-729 and 1003-1004 (level-up foot=36, h=min(156,H-top-foot), tipY/rowY at the screen foot), 1284 (COLLECT at H-22), 1420-1431 (chestLayout CHEST_FOOT); public/js/ui/trophy.js:89 (TAKE NONE at min(H-18,by)); public/js/game/dungeon.js:1790-1796; public/js/game/loadout.js:50-75 (belt) [both critics: art + tech]
- Evidence: out-chrome/cphone-04-levelup-ready.png (tech-extra/lu-footer-phone.png): 'Tap a card to take it · hold a card to banish it' (y~490) is struck through by the plate's bronze rim, BANISH/REROLL sit on the dimmed loadout (y505-530), and the #5a6988 tip is barely legible on snow. c580-04: 'Enter or 1-3 takes a card' over the plate headers (380-780, 385-440). c1920-04: footer over the headers (290-680, 488-540). c1280-07/c1280-09/cphone-07: COLLECT (560-720, 700-722) and its cursor sit on the plate cells. c240-sheet1: 'Plate Armor / LV 3', '+36 GOLD' and COLLECT stacked on both plates. art-extra/cryptmap-bottom.png: the map box cuts the plate rims and headers.
- The overlays treat H as the screen bottom, but the HUD under the 0.55-0.7 dim still draws its plates up to belt.y (H-36, or higher when stacked or on touch). Overlay text and buttons overprint dim UI chrome, so two UI layers fight.
- Fix: Pass belt = g.hud.loadout(W,H) to the overlays and use bottom = belt.y-2: level-up h = min(156, bottom-top-foot); COLLECT y = bottom-16; chestLayout(W, bottom, …); minimap height ≤ bottom-oy-4. Simpler alternative: add a Hud.draw 'bare' option that skips drawLoadout, drawIndicators and the orb labels while g.state is levelup, chest or item, or a trophy overlay is open. Another option is a solid #181425 strip behind the overlay foot. Lift the level-up tip colour from #5a6988 to #8b9bb4.
- Reproduce sceptic (real, defect): Confirmed in pixels and in code. GameScreen.update (gamescreen.js:184) calls g.hud.draw under every overlay except Results. Hud.draw (hud.js:388) always calls drawLoadout. loadoutLayout (loadout.js:50-75) puts the plates at H-3-34 on desktop, at H-25-34 on touch, and about 70 px tall when stacked at 240 wide. The overlays treat H as their bottom: - Level-up: foot=36, h=min(156,H-top-foot), rowY=min(H-18,bottom+18), tipY=rowY-11. - Chest: COLLECT at H-22, and chestLayout gets H. - Trophy: TAKE NONE at min(H-18,by). - Dungeon minimap: mh is held only under H-60, so its box can reach H-24, which is below the belt's top at H-37. What the captures show: - cphone-04 / lu-footer-phone: the plate's bronze top rim runs through 'Tap a card to take it · hold a card to banish it', and BANISH/REROLL sit right on the dimmed WEAPONS/POWERS cells, whose icons and numbers show between the buttons. - c1920-04: 'Enter or 1-4 takes a card' prints over the POWERS header, and WEAPONS peeks out under BANISH. - c580-04: the tip sits on both headers. - c1280-07: COLLECT and its cursor sit on the plate cells. - c240-sheet1: 'Plate Armor LV 3' is on the WEAPONS header, '+36 GOLD' is on POWERS, and COLLECT is below them. - cryptmap-bottom: the map box's lower edge cuts across both plate headers. This is not a capture artefact. The capture forced 6 weapon slots, which only widens the plates. The plates' height and position come from H alone, so any real run gets the same clash. Gameplay is not broken, because the buttons still work. But it is a two-layer UI clash on every level-up and chest screen at every resolution, so 'defect' is the right severity. The critic's main fix (bottom = belt.y-2 for the overlays) is not sound. By my arithmetic from the code (top is about 65 art px), it shrinks the cards a lot: - 633x293 touch: 156 to about 130 px. - 580x216: 114 to about 77 px. - 240x210 stacked belt (y about 137): a stacked card goes from about 30 to about 5 px. All that space would go to keeping a dimmed strip that nobody can use under a modal screen. The chest screen also has no need for the bottom belt: since 28 Oct it redraws the build's slots and the gold counter at full light top-left over the dim (gamescreen.js:1152-1166). The critic's second option, a 'bare' HUD, is the right one, but it should be smaller than written. Indicators and orb labels do not collide with anything here and need no change. Note: gamescreen.js has another session's uncommitted 3-line hunk. Any edit there, or in hud.js, has to be staged around it. Better fix: The smallest fix, with no layout change and no smaller cards: in Hud.draw (public/js/game/hud.js:388), stop drawing the belt plates while a modal card or chest screen is up. Change it to `if (g.state !== 'levelup' && g.state !== 'chest' && g.state !== 'item') drawLoadout(ctx, belt, t);`. TrophyOverlay also sets state 'chest', so this covers TAKE NONE too. Keep the belt variable itself, because drawOrbs, drawIndicators and markerBand still lay out by it. Keep the belt under 'paused': the pause panel stands clear of it (c1280-12). On the chest screen nothing is lost, since ChestOverlay already redraws the build top-left at full light. On the level-up screen the cards carry each rank. For the crypt/dungeon big map, also skip drawLoadout while g.showMap is on. Or clamp the big map in drawMinimap so its height stays at or under belt.y - oy - 4. If the owner wants the dimmed build kept under level-up, the other way is a solid #181425 strip (about 0.9 alpha) from tipY-3 to H behind the level-up foot and COLLECT. Do not shrink the overlays to belt.y. Optional: lift the plain level-up tip colour from #5a6988 to #8b9bb4 (gamescreen.js:988).
- Intent sceptic (real, defect): The finding is real and is not something the owner chose. The bottom WEAPONS/POWERS belt arrived only yesterday (cb5a89b, 2026-11-21, "redesign the combat loadout HUD"). That same commit moved the LEVEL COMPLETE panel above the belt (gamescreen.js:207 uses hud.loadout(W,H).y - bh - 47). Every other HUD piece also keeps clear of it: ultLabelY, drawCryptStrike, drawMapHint and markerBand's keep(belt...). The level-up, chest, trophy and crypt-map overlays are older than the belt and still treat H as the bottom of the screen. No review in reviews/ covers this. The screenshots confirm it: - c1920-04 (1920x1080 full screen, 480x270 art px): the tip and BANISH/REROLL sit on the plate headers. - cphone-04: the touch belt is raised 25 px, and the rim cuts through the tip. - c1280-07 and c240-sheet1: COLLECT, '+36 GOLD' and 'Plate Armor LV 3' are printed over the dimmed plates. My arithmetic from the code agrees. At 480x270 the belt starts at y=233, and the level-up foot sits at tipY≈232 and rowY=243. At 1280x720 nothing overlaps. It only changes how things look. The overlap happens at common sizes (1080p full screen, phones in landscape), so I'd call it a defect, not polish. Of the files a fix touches, only gamescreen.js has the other session's uncommitted work, and that diff is 3 lines and doesn't touch this layout. The critic's main fix is not sound. If the cards end 2 px above belt.y, then at 480x270 the cards shrink from 156 to about 126 px (top≈69). Two things go wrong: 1. A 4-line description (Caltrops wraps to 4 lines there) ends at y+100, but the evolution hint starts at y+h-27≈99, so the text collides. 2. The `h - low` bound (low=28) drops the green change line, e.g. Plate Armor's 'Armor 2 → 3'. At 240x210 the plates stack and belt.y≈137, so the stacked cards get about 3 px each. chestLayout(W, belt.y…) also gives a negative cy there. That fix would need floors and fallbacks, and it would be a larger change inside the WIP file. The critic's own "bare" alternative is smaller and safe at every size. It goes only in hud.js, which has no uncommitted work. It already has a precedent in the same file: drawBanners returns early under levelup, chest, item and paused.
